<div style="break-before: page; margin-top: 6pt; margin-bottom: 12pt"><p style="margin: 0pt">&#160;</p></div><!-- Field: /Page --><p style="margin: 0">&#160;</p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: center"><span style="font-size: 10pt"><span style="font-weight: bold;"><span id="a_007" style="display: inline-block;"></span>CHAMPIONSGATE
ACQUISITION CORPORATION</span></span></p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: center"><span style="font-size: 10pt"><span style="font-weight: bold;">NOTES TO UNAUDITED FINANCIAL STATEMENTS</span></span></p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-weight: bold;">&#160;</span></p>
 <ix:nonNumeric name="us-gaap:OrganizationConsolidationAndPresentationOfFinancialStatementsDisclosureTextBlock" contextRef="cref_1760999556" escape="true" id="ixv-2860"><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-weight: bold;">Note&#160;1 &#8212;&#160; Organization, Business Operation and Going Concern Consideration</span></p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"> ChampionsGate Acquisition Corporation (the &#8220;Company&#8221;) is a blank check company incorporated in the Cayman Islands on <ix:nonNumeric name="dei:EntityIncorporationDateOfIncorporation" contextRef="cref_1760999556" format="ixt:date-monthname-day-year-en" id="ixv-6189">March&#160;27, 2024</ix:nonNumeric> as an exempted company with limited liability. The Company was formed for the purpose of effecting a merger, share exchange, asset acquisition, share purchase, recapitalization, reorganization or similar business combination with one or more businesses or entities (the &#8220;Business Combination&#8221;). The Company&#8217;s efforts to identify a prospective target business will not be limited to a particular industry or geographic location. The Company has elected December&#160;31 as its fiscal year end. </p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">As of March 31, 2026, the Company had not commenced any operations. For the period from March&#160;27, 2024 (inception) through March 31, 2026, the Company&#8217;s efforts have been limited to organizational activities as well as activities related to the IPO (see Note&#160;3). The Company will not generate any operating revenues until after the completion of a Business Combination, at the earliest. The Company will generate non-operating income in the form of dividend and/or interest income from the proceeds derived from the IPO and Private Placement (see Note&#160;4).</p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">The Company&#8217;s management has broad discretion with respect to the specific application of the net proceeds of the IPO and the sale of the Private Placement Units (as defined below), although substantially all of the net proceeds are intended to be applied generally toward consummating a Business Combination. There is no assurance that the Company will be able to complete a Business Combination successfully.</p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">The Company&#8217;s founder and sponsor is ST Sponsor Limited, a Cayman Islands exempted company (the &#8220;Sponsor&#8221;). The Company&#8217;s ability to commence operations is contingent upon obtaining adequate financial resources through the IPO and the Private Placement.</p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"> On May 29, 2025, the Company consummated IPO of <ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:StockIssuedDuringPeriodSharesNewIssues" contextRef="cref_931814617" unitRef="uref_1736812714" scale="0" decimals="0" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" id="ixv-6190">7,475,000</ix:nonFraction> units (including <ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:StockIssuedDuringPeriodSharesNewIssues" contextRef="cref_115978538" unitRef="uref_1736812714" scale="0" decimals="0" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" id="ixv-6191">975,000</ix:nonFraction> units issued upon the full exercise of the over-allotment option, the &#8220;Units&#8221;). Each Unit consists of <ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:StockIssuedDuringPeriodSharesOther" contextRef="cref_2126703736" unitRef="uref_1736812714" scale="0" decimals="0" format="ixt-sec:numwordsen" id="ixv-6192">one</ix:nonFraction> Class A ordinary share (the &#8220;Class A ordinary share&#8221;), $<ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:CommonStockParOrStatedValuePerShare" contextRef="cref_2081405700" unitRef="uref_202783376" scale="0" decimals="4" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" id="ixv-6193">0.0001</ix:nonFraction> par value per share, and <ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:ClassOfWarrantOrRightExercisePriceOfWarrantsOrRights1" contextRef="cref_1517209818" unitRef="uref_202783376" scale="0" decimals="0" format="ixt-sec:numwordsen" id="ixv-6194">one</ix:nonFraction> right (&#8220;Right&#8221;) to receive of one-eighth&#160;of <ix:nonFraction name="chpgu:NumberOfSharesOwnedUponBusinessCombination" contextRef="cref_401266707" unitRef="uref_1736812714" scale="0" decimals="0" format="ixt-sec:numwordsen" id="ixv-6195">one</ix:nonFraction> Class&#160;A ordinary share upon the completion of the initial Business Combination of the Company. The Units were sold at an offering price of $<ix:nonFraction name="chpgu:SaleOfStockNumberOfUnits" contextRef="cref_1811502174" unitRef="uref_202783376" scale="0" decimals="2" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" id="ixv-6196">10.00</ix:nonFraction> per Unit, generating total gross proceeds of $<ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:ProceedsFromIssuanceInitialPublicOffering" contextRef="cref_931814617" unitRef="uref_1995766304" scale="0" decimals="0" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" id="ixv-6197">74,750,000</ix:nonFraction>. </p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"> Simultaneously with the consummation (the &#8220;closing&#8221;) of the IPO and the sale of the Units, the Company consummated the Private Placement of <ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:SaleOfStockNumberOfSharesIssuedInTransaction" contextRef="cref_1669086407" unitRef="uref_1736812714" scale="0" decimals="0" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" id="ixv-6198">230,000</ix:nonFraction> units (the &#8220;Private Placement Units&#8221;) to ST Sponsor Investment LLC (the &#8220;Sponsor HoldCo&#8221;), a Cayman Islands limited liability company which has one member, ST Sponsor Limited, the Company&#8217;s Sponsor, at a price of $<ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:SaleOfStockPricePerShare" contextRef="cref_535909029" unitRef="uref_202783376" scale="0" decimals="2" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" id="ixv-6199">10.00</ix:nonFraction> per Private Placement Unit, generating total proceeds of $<ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:ProceedsFromIssuanceOfPrivatePlacement" contextRef="cref_1669086407" unitRef="uref_1995766304" scale="0" decimals="0" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" id="ixv-6200">2,300,000</ix:nonFraction>, which is described in Note 4. Each Private Placement Unit consists of <ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:StockIssuedDuringPeriodSharesNewIssues" contextRef="cref_1399440341" unitRef="uref_1736812714" scale="0" decimals="0" format="ixt-sec:numwordsen" id="ixv-6201">one</ix:nonFraction> Class A ordinary share, and one Right to receive of one-eighth&#160;of <ix:nonFraction name="chpgu:NumberOfSharesOwnedUponBusinessCombination" contextRef="cref_1748500572" unitRef="uref_1736812714" scale="0" decimals="0" format="ixt-sec:numwordsen" id="ixv-6202">one</ix:nonFraction> Class&#160;A ordinary share upon the completion of the initial Business Combination. </p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"> Transaction costs amounted to $<ix:nonFraction name="chpgu:TransactionCosts" contextRef="cref_1760999556" unitRef="uref_1995766304" scale="0" decimals="0" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" id="ixv-6203">3,259,220</ix:nonFraction>, consisting of $<ix:nonFraction name="chpgu:UnderwritingCommissions" contextRef="cref_1870043804" unitRef="uref_1995766304" scale="0" decimals="0" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" id="ixv-6204">747,500</ix:nonFraction> of underwriting commissions which was paid in cash at the closing date of the IPO, $<ix:nonFraction name="chpgu:DeferredUnderwritingCommission" contextRef="cref_1870043804" unitRef="uref_1995766304" scale="0" decimals="0" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" id="ixv-6205">1,495,000</ix:nonFraction> of deferred underwriting commissions, $<ix:nonFraction name="chpgu:RepresentativeShares" contextRef="cref_1760999556" unitRef="uref_1995766304" scale="0" decimals="0" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" id="ixv-6206">293,020</ix:nonFraction> of the Representative Shares (discussed below), and $<ix:nonFraction name="chpgu:OtherOfferingCosts" contextRef="cref_1760999556" unitRef="uref_1995766304" scale="0" decimals="0" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" id="ixv-6207">723,700</ix:nonFraction> of other offering costs. At the IPO closing date, cash of $<ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:Cash" contextRef="cref_673232712" unitRef="uref_1995766304" scale="0" decimals="0" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" id="ixv-6208">464,339</ix:nonFraction>&#160;was held outside of the trust account (as defined below) and is available for the payment of accrued offering costs and for working capital purposes. </p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"> In conjunction with the IPO, the Company issued to the underwriter <ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:StockIssuedDuringPeriodSharesNewIssues" contextRef="cref_1426676510" unitRef="uref_1736812714" scale="0" decimals="0" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" id="ixv-6209">112,125</ix:nonFraction> Class A ordinary shares for no consideration (the &#8220;Representative Shares&#8221;). The fair value of the Representative Shares accounted for as compensation under Financial Accounting Standards Board (&#8220;FASB&#8221;) Accounting Standards Codification (&#8220;ASC&#8221;) 718, &#8220;Compensation &#8211; Stock Compensation&#8221; (&#8220;ASC 718&#8221;) is included in the offering costs. The estimated fair value of the Representative Shares as of the IPO date totaled $<ix:nonFraction name="chpgu:RepresentativeShares" contextRef="cref_1870043804" unitRef="uref_1995766304" scale="0" decimals="0" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" id="ixv-6210">293,020</ix:nonFraction>. </p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p><ix:exclude><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"></p><!-- Field: Page; Sequence: 7; Value: 1 --><div style="margin-top: 12pt; margin-bottom: 6pt; border-bottom: Black 1.5pt solid"><p style="font: normal 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; text-align: center; margin-top: 0pt; margin-bottom: 0pt">&#8239;<!-- Field: Sequence; Type: Arabic; Name: PageNo -->5<!-- Field: /Sequence --></p></div><div style="break-before: page; margin-top: 6pt; margin-bottom: 12pt"><p style="margin: 0pt">&#160;</p></div><!-- Field: /Page --><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p></ix:exclude><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"> The Company&#8217;s initial Business Combination must occur with one or more target businesses that together have an aggregate fair market value of at least <ix:nonFraction name="chpgu:PercentageOfAggregateFairMarketValue" contextRef="cref_1760999556" unitRef="uref_466989387" scale="-2" decimals="2" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" id="ixv-6211">80</ix:nonFraction>% of the value of the trust account (excluding any deferred underwriters&#8217; fees and taxes payable on the income earned on the trust account) at the time of the agreement to enter into the initial Business Combination. The Company will complete its initial Business Combination only if the post-transaction company in which its public shareholders own shares will own or acquire <ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:BusinessAcquisitionPercentageOfVotingInterestsAcquired" contextRef="cref_1432046759" unitRef="uref_466989387" scale="-2" decimals="2" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" id="ixv-6212">50</ix:nonFraction>% or more of the outstanding voting securities of the target or otherwise acquires a controlling interest in the target sufficient for it not to be required to register as an investment company under the Investment Company Act. There is no assurance that the Company will be able to complete a Business Combination successfully. </p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"> Upon the closing of the IPO, management has agreed that at least $<ix:nonFraction name="chpgu:PricePerUnitSoldOrTransferredToTrustAccount" contextRef="cref_1802993292" unitRef="uref_202783376" scale="0" decimals="2" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" id="ixv-6213">10.05</ix:nonFraction> per Unit sold in the IPO will be held into a U.S.-based trust account (&#8220;trust account&#8221;). The funds held in the trust account will be invested only in U.S.&#160;government treasury bills with a maturity of <ix:nonNumeric name="chpgu:MaturityPeriodOfFundsHeldInTheTrustAccount" contextRef="cref_1802993292" format="ixt-sec:durday" id="ixv-6214">185</ix:nonNumeric>&#160;days or less, or in money market funds meeting the applicable conditions of Rule&#160;2a-7 promulgated under the Investment Company Act which invest solely in direct U.S.&#160;government treasury. Except with respect to divided and/or interest earned on the funds held in the trust account that may be released to the Company to pay the Company&#8217;s tax obligation, if any, the proceeds from the IPO and the sale of the Private Placement Units that are deposited and held in the trust account will not be released from the trust account until the earliest to occur of (i)&#160;the completion of the Company&#8217;s initial Business Combination, (ii)&#160;the redemption of any public shares properly tendered in connection with a shareholder vote to amend the company&#8217;s second amended and restated memorandum and articles of association to (A)&#160;modify the substance or timing of obligation to redeem <ix:nonFraction name="chpgu:ConditionalModificationOnRedemptionObligationPercentageOfPublicShares" contextRef="cref_1760999556" unitRef="uref_466989387" scale="-2" decimals="2" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" id="ixv-6215">100</ix:nonFraction>% of the Company&#8217;s public shares if the Company does not complete the Company&#8217;s initial Business Combination within 18&#160;months from the closing of the IPO. (The Company has eighteen (18) months after the Closing Date to complete its initial Business Combination unless the Company and the Sponsor and/or designees elect to extend this initial time period pursuant to the following scenarios: (x) in the event that the Company executes a definitive agreement for an initial Business Combination within 18 months after the Closing Date, it will automatically receive an additional three months to consummate the initial Business Combination or (y) the Company and the Sponsor and/or designees may extend that initial time period two times by an additional three months each time up to for a total period of up to 24 months from the Closing Date,&#160; or 27&#160;months from the closing of the IPO if the condition in scenario (x) is met) (the &#8220;Combination Deadline&#8221;) provided that the Company&#8217;s sponsor and/or designees must deposit (the &#8220;extension loans&#8221;) into the trust account for each three&#160;months extension, $<ix:nonFraction name="chpgu:AmountHeldInTrustAccount" contextRef="cref_1802993292" unitRef="uref_1995766304" scale="0" decimals="0" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" id="ixv-6216">747,500</ix:nonFraction>, up to an aggregate of $<ix:nonFraction name="chpgu:OverallotmentOptionExercisableValue" contextRef="cref_1760999556" unitRef="uref_1995766304" scale="0" decimals="0" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" id="ixv-6217">1,495,000</ix:nonFraction>, on or prior to the date of the applicable deadline. or (B)&#160;with respect to any other provision relating to shareholders&#8217; rights or pre-business combination activity and (iii)&#160;the redemption of all of public shares if the company are unable to complete their initial Business Combination before the Combination Deadline, subject to applicable law. In no other circumstances will a public shareholder have any right or interest of any kind to or in the trust account. The proceeds deposited in the trust account could become subject to the claims of the Company&#8217;s creditors, if any, which could have priority over the claims of the public shareholders. </p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">The Company will provide its public shareholders with the opportunity to redeem all or a portion of their public shares upon the completion of the Business Combination either (i)&#160;in connection with a shareholder meeting called to approve the Business Combination or (ii)&#160;by means of a tender offer.</p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"> The ordinary shares subject to redemption will be accredited to the redemption value and classified as temporary equity upon the completion of the IPO, in accordance with ASC Topic&#160;480, &#8220;Distinguishing Liabilities from Equity.&#8221; The Company has determined not to consummate any Business Combination unless the Company has net tangible assets of at least $<ix:nonFraction name="chpgu:NetTangibleAssets" contextRef="cref_1802993292" unitRef="uref_1995766304" scale="0" decimals="0" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" id="ixv-6218">5,000,001</ix:nonFraction> upon such consummation in order to avoid being subject to Rule&#160;419 promulgated under the Securities Act. </p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"> The Company will have until the Combination Deadline to complete its initial Business Combination. The Company will: (i)&#160;cease all operations except for the purpose of winding up; (ii)&#160;as promptly as reasonably possible but not more than ten&#160;business days thereafter, redeem the public shares, at a per-share&#160;price, payable in cash, equal to the aggregate amount then on deposit in the trust account, including interest earned on the funds held in the trust account and not previously released to the Company to pay the Company&#8217;s franchise and income taxes, if any (less up to $<ix:nonFraction name="chpgu:AmountOfInterestToPayDissolutionExpense" contextRef="cref_1760999556" unitRef="uref_1995766304" scale="0" decimals="0" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" id="ixv-6219">100,000</ix:nonFraction> of interest to pay dissolution expenses) divided by the number of the then-issued&#160;and outstanding public shares, which redemption will completely extinguish public shareholders&#8217; rights as shareholders (including the right to receive further liquidation distributions, if any); and (iii)&#160;as promptly as reasonably possible following such redemption, subject to the approval of its remaining shareholders and its board of directors, liquidate and dissolve, subject in each case to the Company&#8217;s obligations under Cayman Islands law to provide for claims of creditors and the requirements of other applicable law. The Sponsor HoldCo and each member of management team have entered into an agreement with the Company, pursuant to which they have agreed to waive their rights to liquidating distributions from the trust account with respect to any insider shares (as defined in Note 5) they hold if the Company fails to consummate an initial Business Combination before the Combination Deadline. </p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p></ix:exclude><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"> The Sponsor has agreed that it will be liable to the Company if and to the extent any claims by a third party for services rendered or products sold to the Company, or a prospective target business with which the Company has entered into a written letter of intent, confidentiality or similar agreement or Business Combination agreement, reduce the amount of funds in the trust account to below the lesser of (i)&#160;$<ix:nonFraction name="chpgu:PublicSharePrice1" contextRef="cref_1870043804" unitRef="uref_202783376" scale="0" decimals="2" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" id="ixv-6220">10.05</ix:nonFraction> per public share and (ii)&#160;the actual amount per public share held in the trust account as of the date of the liquidation of the trust account, if less than $<ix:nonFraction name="chpgu:PublicSharePrice1" contextRef="cref_1760999556" unitRef="uref_202783376" scale="0" decimals="2" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" id="ixv-6221">10.05</ix:nonFraction> per share due to reductions in the value of the trust assets, less taxes payable, provided that such liability will not apply to any claims by a third party or prospective target business who executed a waiver of any and all rights to the monies held in the trust account (whether or not such waiver is enforceable) nor will it apply to any claims under the Company&#8217;s indemnity of the underwriters of this IPO against certain liabilities, including liabilities under the Securities Act. However, the Company has not asked the Sponsor to reserve for such indemnification obligations, nor have the Company independently verified whether the Company&#8217;s Sponsor has sufficient funds to satisfy its indemnity obligations and believe that the Sponsor&#8217;s only assets are securities of the company. Therefore, it cannot be assured that that the Sponsor would be able to satisfy those obligations. None of the officers or directors will indemnify the Company for claims by third parties including, without limitation, claims by vendors and prospective target businesses. </p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-weight: bold;">&#160;</span></p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-weight: bold;">Going Concern Consideration</span></p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"> As of March 31, 2026, the Company had a working capital deficit of $<ix:nonFraction name="chpgu:WorkingCapitalDeficiency" contextRef="cref_1802993292" unitRef="uref_1995766304" scale="0" decimals="0" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" id="ixv-6222">182,396</ix:nonFraction>. The Company expects to incur significant costs in pursuit of its acquisition plans. These conditions raise substantial doubt about the Company&#8217;s ability to continue as a going concern within one year after the date that the unaudited financial statements are issued. Management&#8217;s plans to address this need for capital through the Working Capital Loans, as defined below (see Note 5). In addition, if the Company is unable to complete a Business Combination before the Combination Deadline, the Company&#8217;s board of directors would proceed to commence a voluntary liquidation and thereby a formal dissolution of the Company. There is no assurance that the Company&#8217;s plans to raise capital or to consummate a Business Combination will be successful or successful within the required period. As a result, management has determined that such additional condition also raise substantial doubt about the Company&#8217;s ability to continue as a going concern. The unaudited financial statements do not include any adjustments that might result from the Company&#8217;s inability to consummate the Business Combination to continue as a going concern. </p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-weight: bold;">&#160;</span></p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-weight: bold;">Risks and Uncertainties</span></p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">In February&#160;2022, the military action was commenced by the Russian Federation and Belarus in the country of Ukraine and related economic sanctions. The military conflict has continued as of the date of this filing. As a result of the military actions, the Company&#8217;s ability to consummate a Business Combination, or the operations of a target business with which the Company ultimately consummates a Business Combination, may be materially and adversely affected. In addition, the Company&#8217;s ability to consummate a transaction may be dependent on the ability to raise equity and debt financing which may be impacted by these events, including as a result of increased market volatility, or decreased market liquidity in third-party financing being unavailable on terms acceptable to the Company or at all. The impact of this action and related sanctions on the world economy and the specific impact on the Company&#8217;s financial position, results of operations and/or ability to consummate a Business Combination are not yet determinable. The unaudited financial statements do not include any adjustments that might result from the outcome of this uncertainty.</p></ix:nonNumeric> <p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-weight: bold;">&#160;</span></p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-weight: bold;"></span></p><!-- Field: Page; Sequence: 9; Value: 1 --><div style="margin-top: 12pt; margin-bottom: 6pt; border-bottom: Black 1.5pt solid"><p style="font: normal 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; text-align: center; margin-top: 0pt; margin-bottom: 0pt">&#8239;<!-- Field: Sequence; Type: Arabic; Name: PageNo -->7<!-- Field: /Sequence --></p></div><div style="break-before: page; margin-top: 6pt; margin-bottom: 12pt"><p style="margin: 0pt">&#160;</p></div><!-- Field: /Page --><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-weight: bold;">&#160;</span></p>
 <ix:nonNumeric name="us-gaap:SignificantAccountingPoliciesTextBlock" contextRef="cref_1760999556" escape="true" id="ixv-2927"><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-weight: bold;">Note&#160;2 &#8212;&#160; Significant Accounting Policies</span></p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-weight: bold;">&#160;</span></p><ix:nonNumeric name="us-gaap:BasisOfAccountingPolicyPolicyTextBlock" contextRef="cref_1760999556" escape="true" id="ixv-2932"><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-weight: bold;">Basis of Presentation</span></p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">The accompanying unaudited financial statements are presented in conformity with accounting principles generally accepted in the United&#160;States of America (&#8220;US GAAP&#8221;) and pursuant to the rules and regulations of the U.S.&#160;Securities and Exchange Commission (&#8220;SEC&#8221;). The interim financial information provided is unaudited but includes all adjustments which management considers necessary for the fair presentation of the results for the period. The information included in this Form 10-Q should be read in conjunction with information included in the Company&#8217;s annual report on Form 10-K for the year ended December 31, 2025, filed with the SEC on April 10, 2026. Operating results for the interim period ended March 31, 2026 are not necessarily indicative of the results that may be expected for the fiscal year ending December 31, 2026.</p></ix:nonNumeric><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-weight: bold;">&#160;</span></p><ix:nonNumeric name="chpgu:EmergingGrowthCompanyPolicyTextBlock" contextRef="cref_1760999556" escape="true" id="ixv-2939"><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-weight: bold;">Emerging Growth Company</span></p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">The Company is an &#8220;emerging growth company,&#8221; as defined in Section&#160;2(a)&#160;of the Securities Act&#160;of&#160;1933, as amended, (the &#8220;Securities Act&#8221;), as modified by the Jumpstart Our Business Startups Act&#160;of&#160;2012 (the &#8220;JOBS Act&#8221;), and it may take advantage of certain exemptions from various reporting requirements that are applicable to other public companies that are not emerging growth companies including, but not limited to, not being required to comply with the auditor attestation requirements of Section&#160;404 of the Sarbanes-Oxley Act, reduced disclosure obligations regarding executive compensation in its periodic reports and proxy statements, and exemptions from the requirements of holding a nonbinding advisory vote on executive compensation and shareholder approval of any golden parachute payments not previously approved.</p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">Further, Section&#160;102(b)(1)&#160;of the JOBS Act exempts emerging growth companies from being required to comply with new or revised financial accounting standards until private companies (that is, those that have not had a Securities Act registration statement declared effective or do not have a class of securities registered under the Securities Exchange&#160;Act of 1934, as amended) are required to comply with the new or revised financial accounting standards. The JOBS Act provides that a company can elect to opt out of the extended transition period and comply with the requirements that apply to non-emerging growth companies but any such an election to opt out is irrevocable. The Company has elected not to opt out of such extended transition period which means that when a standard is issued or revised and it has different application dates for public or private companies, the Company, as an emerging growth company, can adopt the new or revised standard at the time private companies adopt the new or revised standard. This may make comparison of the Company&#8217;s unaudited financial statements with another public company which is neither an emerging growth company nor an emerging growth company which has opted out of using the extended transition period difficult or impossible because of the potential differences in accounting standards used.</p></ix:nonNumeric><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-weight: bold;">&#160;</span></p><ix:nonNumeric name="us-gaap:UseOfEstimates" contextRef="cref_1760999556" escape="true" id="ixv-2948"><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-weight: bold;">Use of Estimates</span></p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">The preparation of the unaudited financial statements in conformity with US GAAP requires management to make estimates and assumptions that affect the reported amounts of assets and liabilities and disclosure of contingent assets and liabilities at the date of the unaudited financial statements and the reported amounts of expenses during the reporting period. Actual results could differ from those estimates.</p></ix:nonNumeric><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-weight: bold;">&#160;</span></p><ix:nonNumeric name="us-gaap:CashAndCashEquivalentsPolicyTextBlock" contextRef="cref_1760999556" escape="true" id="ixv-2955"><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-weight: bold;">Cash and Cash Equivalents</span></p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"> The Company considers all short-term investments with an original maturity of three&#160;months or less when purchased to be cash equivalents. The Company had $<ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:Cash" contextRef="cref_1802993292" unitRef="uref_1995766304" scale="0" decimals="0" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" id="ixv-6223">16,862</ix:nonFraction> and $<ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:Cash" contextRef="cref_1993779210" unitRef="uref_1995766304" scale="0" decimals="0" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" id="ixv-6224">17,251</ix:nonFraction> of cash held in bank accounts as of March 31,2026 and December 31,2025, respectively. </p></ix:nonNumeric><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p><ix:nonNumeric name="chpgu:CashAndInvestmentsHeldInTrustAccountPolicyTextBlock" contextRef="cref_1760999556" escape="true" id="ixv-2961"><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-weight: bold;">Investments Held in Trust Account</span></p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"> As of March 31, 2026 and December 31, 2025, substantially all of the assets of $<ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:AssetsHeldInTrustCurrent" contextRef="cref_1802993292" unitRef="uref_1995766304" scale="0" decimals="0" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" id="ixv-6225">77,578,527</ix:nonFraction>&#160;and $<ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:AssetsHeldInTrustCurrent" contextRef="cref_1993779210" unitRef="uref_1995766304" scale="0" decimals="0" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" id="ixv-6226">76,902,330</ix:nonFraction>&#160;held in the trust account, respectively, are invested primarily in money market funds. These investments are presented on the balance sheet at fair value at the end of each reporting period. Earnings on these investments are included in interest and dividends income in the accompanying statements of operations and are automatically reinvested. The fair value for these investments is determined by using quoted market prices in active markets. The interest and dividend income on investments held in trust account was $<ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:InvestmentIncomeInterestAndDividend" contextRef="cref_1760999556" unitRef="uref_1995766304" scale="0" decimals="0" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" id="ixv-6227">676,197</ix:nonFraction> and $<span style="-sec-ix-hidden:fc_1023641468">0</span> for the three months ended March 31, 2026 and 2025, respectively. </p></ix:nonNumeric><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p><ix:exclude><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"></p><!-- Field: Page; Sequence: 10; Value: 1 --><div style="margin-top: 12pt; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"> </p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"> <span style="background-color: white">Financial instruments that potentially subject the Company to concentrations of credit risk consist of cash accounts in a financial institution, which, at times, may exceed the Federal Depository Insurance Coverage (&#8220;FDIC&#8221;) of $<ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:CashFDICInsuredAmount" contextRef="cref_1802993292" unitRef="uref_1995766304" scale="0" decimals="0" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" id="ixv-6228">250,000</ix:nonFraction>. The Company has not experienced losses on the account.</span> <span style="font-weight: bold;"></span> </p></ix:nonNumeric><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p><ix:nonNumeric name="us-gaap:EarningsPerSharePolicyTextBlock" contextRef="cref_1760999556" escape="true" id="ixv-2986"><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-weight: bold;">Net Income (Loss) Per Share</span></p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-weight: bold;">&#160;</span></p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"> The Company complies with accounting and disclosure requirements of FASB ASC 260, &#8220;Earnings Per Share&#8221;. Net income (loss) per ordinary share is computed by dividing net income (loss) by the weighted average number of ordinary shares outstanding for the period. Remeasurement of carrying value to redemption value of redeemable ordinary shares is excluded from income (loss) per share as the redemption value approximates fair value. For the three months ended March 31, 2026, the Company has not considered the effect of the Rights included in the IPO and Private Placement Units in the calculation of diluted net income (loss) per share, since the conversion of the Rights is contingent upon the occurrence of future events and the inclusion of such Rights would be anti-dilutive and the Company did not have any other dilutive securities and other contracts that could, potentially, be exercised or converted into ordinary shares and then share in the earnings of the Company.&#160;<ix:nonNumeric name="us-gaap:ScheduleOfEarningsPerShareBasicAndDilutedTableTextBlock" contextRef="cref_1760999556" continuedAt="continuation_1600554854" escape="true" id="ixv-6229">As a result, diluted income (loss) per share is the same as basic income (loss) per share for the period presented.</ix:nonNumeric> </p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p><ix:nonNumeric name="us-gaap:FairValueMeasurementPolicyPolicyTextBlock" contextRef="cref_1760999556" escape="true" id="ixv-3193"><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-weight: bold;">Fair Value of Financial Instruments</span></p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">The fair value of the Company&#8217;s assets and liabilities, which qualify as financial instruments under ASC&#160;820, &#8220;Fair Value Measurements and Disclosures&#8221; (&#8220;ASC 820&#8221;), approximates the carrying amounts represented in the accompanying balance sheet, primarily due to their short-term nature.</p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;&#160;</p><ix:exclude><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"></p><!-- Field: Page; Sequence: 11; Value: 1 --><div style="margin-top: 12pt; margin-bottom: 6pt; border-bottom: Black 1.5pt solid"><p style="font: normal 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; text-align: center; margin-top: 0pt; margin-bottom: 0pt">&#8239;<!-- Field: Sequence; Type: Arabic; Name: PageNo -->9<!-- Field: /Sequence --></p></div><div style="break-before: page; margin-top: 6pt; margin-bottom: 12pt"><p style="margin: 0pt">&#160;</p></div><!-- Field: /Page --><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p></ix:exclude><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">The Company applies ASC 820, which establishes a framework for measuring fair value and clarifies the definition of fair value within that framework. ASC 820 defines fair value as an exit price, which is the price that would be received for an asset or paid to transfer a liability in the Company&#8217;s principal or most advantageous market in an orderly transaction between market participants on the measurement date. The fair value hierarchy established in ASC 820 generally requires an entity to maximize the use of observable inputs and minimize the use of unobservable inputs when measuring fair value. Observable inputs reflect the assumptions that market participants would use in pricing the asset or liability and are developed based on market data obtained&#160;from sources independent of the reporting entity. Unobservable inputs reflect the entity&#8217;s own assumptions based on market data and the entity&#8217;s judgments about the assumptions that market participants would use in pricing the asset or liability and are to be developed based on the best information available in the circumstances.</p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0 0pt 0.5in; text-align: justify; text-indent: -0.25in">&#160;</p><table cellpadding="0" cellspacing="0" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; width: 100%"> <tbody><tr style="vertical-align: top"><td style="width: 24px">&#160;</td> <td style="width: 24px"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#9679;</span></td> <td style="text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">Level 1 &#8212; Assets and liabilities with unadjusted, quoted prices listed on active market exchanges. Inputs to the fair value measurement are observable inputs, such as quoted prices in active markets for identical assets or liabilities.</span></td> </tr></tbody></table><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0 0pt 0.5in; text-align: justify; text-indent: -0.25in">&#160;</p><table cellpadding="0" cellspacing="0" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; width: 100%"> <tbody><tr style="vertical-align: top"><td style="width: 24px">&#160;</td> <td style="width: 24px"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#9679;</span></td> <td style="text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">Level 2 &#8212; Inputs to the fair value measurement are determined using prices for recently traded assets and liabilities with similar underlying terms, as well as direct or indirect observable inputs, such as interest rates and yield curves that are observable at commonly quoted intervals.</span></td> </tr></tbody></table><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0 0pt 0.5in; text-align: justify; text-indent: -0.25in">&#160;</p><table cellpadding="0" cellspacing="0" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; width: 100%"> <tbody><tr style="vertical-align: top"><td style="width: 24px">&#160;</td> <td style="width: 24px"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#9679;</span></td> <td style="text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">Level 3 &#8212; Inputs to the fair value measurement are unobservable inputs, such as estimates, assumptions, and valuation techniques when little or no market data exists for the assets or liabilities.</span></td> </tr></tbody></table><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"> The public Rights have been classified within shareholders&#8217; deficit and will not require remeasurement after issuance. The public Rights were classified within Level 3 of the fair value hierarchy at the measurement dates due to the use of unobservable inputs inherent in assumptions related to the market adjustments as noted below. <ix:nonNumeric name="us-gaap:FairValueAssetsMeasuredOnNonrecurringBasisValuationTechniquesTextBlock" contextRef="cref_1760999556" continuedAt="continuation_1714580737" escape="true" id="ixv-6252">The following table presents the quantitative information regarding market assumptions used in the valuation of the public Rights:</ix:nonNumeric> </p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-weight: bold;">Class&#160;A Ordinary Shares Subject to Possible Redemption</span></p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"> The Company accounts for its Class&#160;A ordinary shares subject to possible redemption in accordance with the guidance in ASC Topic&#160;480, &#8220;Distinguishing Liabilities from Equity&#8221; (&#8220;ASC&#160;480&#8221;). Ordinary shares subject to mandatory redemption (if any) will be classified as a liability instrument and will be measured at fair value. Conditionally redeemable ordinary shares (including ordinary shares that features redemption rights that are either within the control of the holder or subject to redemption upon the occurrence of uncertain events not solely within the Company&#8217;s control) will be classified as temporary equity. At all other times, ordinary shares will be classified as shareholders&#8217; equity. In accordance with ASC&#160;480-10-S99, the Company classifies the Class&#160;A ordinary shares subject to redemption outside of permanent equity as the redemption provisions are not solely within the control of the Company. Given that the <ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:TemporaryEquitySharesIssued" contextRef="cref_1875629000" unitRef="uref_1736812714" scale="0" decimals="0" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" id="ixv-6267">7,475,000</ix:nonFraction> Class&#160;A ordinary shares sold as part of the Units&#160;in the IPO were issued with other freestanding instruments (i.e., Rights), the initial carrying value of Class&#160;A ordinary shares classified as temporary equity has been allocated to the proceeds determined in accordance with ASC&#160;470-20. If it is probable that the equity instrument will become redeemable, the Company has the option to either (i)&#160;accrete changes in the redemption value over the period from the date of issuance (or from the date that it becomes probable that the instrument will become redeemable, if later) to the earliest redemption date of the instrument or (ii)&#160;recognize changes in the redemption value immediately as they occur and adjust the carrying amount of the instrument to equal the redemption value at the end of each reporting period. The Company has elected to recognize the changes in the redemption value immediately as they occur and adjust the carrying amount of the instrument to equal the redemption value at the end of each reporting period. </p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p><ix:nonNumeric name="us-gaap:TemporaryEquityTableTextBlock" contextRef="cref_1760999556" escape="true" id="ixv-3467"><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-weight: bold;">Stock Compensation</span></p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">The Company accounts for stock-based compensation expense in accordance with ASC&#160;718, &#8220;Compensation&#160;&#8212;&#160;Stock Compensation&#8221; (&#8220;ASC&#160;718&#8221;). Under ASC&#160;718, stock-based compensation associated with equity-classified awards is measured at fair value upon the grant date and recognized over the requisite service period. To the extent a stock-based award is subject to a performance condition, the amount of expense recorded in a given period, if any, reflects an assessment of the probability of achieving such performance condition, with compensation recognized once the event is deemed probable to occur. Forfeitures are recognized as incurred.</p></ix:nonNumeric><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-weight: bold;">&#160;</span></p><ix:exclude><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-weight: bold;"></span></p><!-- Field: Page; Sequence: 13; Value: 1 --><div style="margin-top: 12pt; margin-bottom: 6pt; border-bottom: Black 1.5pt solid"><p style="font: normal 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; text-align: center; margin-top: 0pt; margin-bottom: 0pt">&#8239;<!-- Field: Sequence; Type: Arabic; Name: PageNo -->11<!-- Field: /Sequence --></p></div><div style="break-before: page; margin-top: 6pt; margin-bottom: 12pt"><p style="margin: 0pt">&#160;</p></div><!-- Field: /Page --><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-weight: bold;">&#160;</span></p></ix:exclude><ix:nonNumeric name="us-gaap:IncomeTaxPolicyTextBlock" contextRef="cref_1760999556" escape="true" id="ixv-3563"><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-weight: bold;">Income Taxes</span></p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">The Company accounts for income taxes under ASC&#160;740, &#8220;Income Taxes&#8221; (&#8220;ASC&#160;740&#8221;). ASC&#160;740 requires the recognition of deferred tax assets and liabilities for both the expected impact of differences between the unaudited financial statement and tax basis of assets and liabilities and for the expected future tax benefit to be derived from tax loss and tax credit carry forwards. ASC&#160;740 additionally requires a valuation allowance to be established when it is more likely than not that all or a portion of deferred tax assets will not be realized.</p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">ASC&#160;740 also clarifies the accounting for uncertainty in income taxes recognized in an enterprise&#8217;s financial statements and prescribes a recognition threshold and measurement process for financial statement recognition and measurement of a tax position taken or expected to be taken in a tax return. For those benefits to be recognized, a tax position must be more-likely-than-not to be sustained upon examination by taxing authorities. ASC&#160;740 also provides guidance on derecognition, classification, interest and penalties, accounting in interim period, disclosure and transition. Based on the Company&#8217;s evaluation, it has been concluded that there are no significant uncertain tax positions requiring recognition in the Company&#8217;s unaudited financial statements.</p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"> The Company recognizes accrued interest and penalties related to unrecognized tax benefits as income tax expense. There were <span style="-sec-ix-hidden:fc_825786952"><span style="-sec-ix-hidden:fc_824626941">no</span></span> unrecognized tax benefits and <span style="-sec-ix-hidden:fc_1880947981"><span style="-sec-ix-hidden:fc_715022210">no</span></span> amounts accrued for interest and penalties as of March 31, 2026 and December 31, 2025. The Company is currently not aware of any issues under review that could result in significant payments, accruals or material deviation from its position. </p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">There is currently no taxation imposed on income by the Government of the Cayman Islands. The Company is considered to be an exempted Cayman Islands Company and is presently not subject to income taxes or income tax filing requirements in the Cayman Islands or the United&#160;States. Consequently, income taxes are not reflected in the Company&#8217;s unaudited financial statements.</p></ix:nonNumeric><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-weight: bold;">&#160;</span></p><ix:nonNumeric name="chpgu:RelatedPartiesPolicyPolicyTextBlock" contextRef="cref_1760999556" escape="true" id="ixv-3580"><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-weight: bold;">Related Parties</span></p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="background-color: white">Parties, which can be a corporation or individual, are considered to be related if the Company has the ability, directly or indirectly, to control the other party or exercise significant influence over the other party in making financial and operational decisions. Companies are also considered to be related if they are subject to common control or common significant influence.</span></p></ix:nonNumeric><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p><ix:nonNumeric name="us-gaap:NewAccountingPronouncementsPolicyPolicyTextBlock" contextRef="cref_1760999556" escape="true" id="ixv-3587"><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-weight: bold;">Recent Accounting Pronouncements</span></p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">Management does not believe that any recently issued, but not effective, accounting standards, if currently adopted, would have a material effect on the Company&#8217;s unaudited financial statements.</p></ix:nonNumeric><ix:exclude><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-weight: bold;">&#160;</span></p></ix:exclude></ix:nonNumeric>
 <ix:nonNumeric name="chpgu:InitialPublicOfferingTextBlock" contextRef="cref_1760999556" escape="true" id="ixv-3595"><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-weight: bold;">Note&#160;3 &#8212;&#160;Initial Public Offering</span></p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"> On May 29, 2025, the Company sold <ix:nonFraction name="chpgu:NumberOfUnitsIssuedDuringThePeriod" contextRef="cref_502901582" unitRef="uref_1736812714" scale="0" decimals="0" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" id="ixv-6276">7,475,000</ix:nonFraction> Units (including <ix:nonFraction name="chpgu:NumberOfUnitsIssuedDuringThePeriod" contextRef="cref_1518117596" unitRef="uref_1736812714" scale="0" decimals="0" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" id="ixv-6277">975,000</ix:nonFraction> Units issued upon the full exercise of the over-allotment option) in its IPO. Each Unit has an offering price of $<ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:SharesIssuedPricePerShare" contextRef="cref_2081405700" unitRef="uref_202783376" scale="0" decimals="2" format="ixt:num-dot-decimal" id="ixv-6278">10.00</ix:nonFraction> and consists of <ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:StockIssuedDuringPeriodSharesOther" contextRef="cref_502901582" unitRef="uref_1736812714" scale="0" decimals="0" format="ixt-sec:numwordsen" id="ixv-6279">one</ix:nonFraction> share of the Company&#8217;s Class&#160;A ordinary share and one Right. Each Right entitles the holder thereof to receive one-eighth of <ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:StockIssuedDuringPeriodSharesOther" contextRef="cref_2126703736" unitRef="uref_1736812714" scale="0" decimals="0" format="ixt-sec:numwordsen" id="ixv-6280">one</ix:nonFraction> Class&#160;A ordinary share upon completion of the Company&#8217;s initial Business Combination. The Company will not issue fractional shares. As a result, the holder must hold rights in multiples of 8 in order to receive shares for all of their rights upon closing of a Business Combination. </p><ix:exclude><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-weight: bold;">&#160;</span></p></ix:exclude></ix:nonNumeric>

In the event the Company will not be the surviving company upon completion of the Company&#8217;s initial Business Combination, each right will automatically be converted to receive the kind and amount of securities or properties of the surviving entity that each one-eighth of one Class&#160;A ordinary share underlying each right is entitled to upon consummation of the Business Combination subject to any dissenter rights under the applicable law. The Company will not issue fractional shares in connection with a conversion of rights. Fractional shares will either be rounded down to the nearest whole share or otherwise addressed in accordance with the applicable provisions of the Companies Act and any other applicable Cayman Islands law. As a result, you must hold rights in multiples of eight in order to receive shares for all of your Class&#160;A ordinary shares underlying the rights upon closing of a Business Combination. If the Company are unable to complete an initial Business Combination within the required time period and the Company redeem the public shares for the funds held in the trust account, holders of rights will not receive any of such funds for their rights and the rights will expire worthless. The Company shall reserve such amount of its profits or share premium in order to pay up the par value of each share issuable in respect of the rights. </p><ix:exclude><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p></ix:exclude></ix:nonNumeric>
 <ix:nonNumeric name="us-gaap:SegmentReportingDisclosureTextBlock" contextRef="cref_1760999556" escape="true" id="ixv-3815"><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-weight: bold;">Note 8 &#8212; Segment Information</span></p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">ASC Topic 280, &#8220;Segment Reporting,&#8221; establishes standards for companies to report in their unaudited financial statement information about operating segments, products, services, geographic areas, and major customers. Operating segments are defined as components of an enterprise for which separate financial information is available that is regularly evaluated by the Company&#8217;s chief operating decision maker, or group, in deciding how to allocate resources and assess performance.</p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"> <ix:nonNumeric name="us-gaap:SegmentReportingCodmProfitLossMeasureHowUsedDescription" contextRef="cref_1760999556" id="ixv-3822">The Company&#8217;s chief operating decision maker has been identified as the <span style="-sec-ix-hidden:fc_857305224">Chief Executive Officer</span> (&#8220;CODM&#8221;), who reviews the operating results for the Company as a whole to make decisions about allocating resources and assessing financial performance.</ix:nonNumeric> Accordingly, management has determined that the Company only has <span style="-sec-ix-hidden:fc_1936091844">one</span> operating segment. </p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p><ix:exclude><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"></p><!-- Field: Page; Sequence: 18; Value: 1 --><div style="margin-top: 12pt; MANAGEMENT&#8217;S DISCUSSION AND ANALYSIS
OF FINANCIAL CONDITION AND RESULTS OF OPERATIONS</span></p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify; text-indent: 0.25in">References in this report
(the &#8220;Quarterly Report&#8221;) to &#8220;we,&#8221; &#8220;us&#8221; or the &#8220;Company&#8221; refer to&#160;ChampionsGate Acquisition
Corporation. References to our &#8220;management&#8221; or our &#8220;management team&#8221; refer to our officers and directors, and
references to the &#8220;Sponsor&#8221; refer to&#160;ST Sponsor Limited and the &#8220;Sponsor HoldCo&#8221; refer to ST Sponsor Investment
LLC. The following discussion and analysis of the Company&#8217;s financial condition and results of operations should be read in conjunction
with the unaudited financial statements and the notes thereto contained elsewhere in this Quarterly Report. Certain information contained
in the discussion and analysis set forth below includes forward-looking statements that involve risks and uncertainties.</p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify">&#160;</p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-weight: bold;">Special Note Regarding Forward-Looking Statements</span></p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><i>&#160;</i></p><p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0; text-align: justify; text-indent: 0.25in"><i>This Quarterly Report
